Excellent results of our method confirm the shared linear structure of learned avatar representations and enable highly efficient and accurate animation at frame rates reaching up to 60fps on mobile devices. Project page: https://ramazan793.github.io/gala/

</details>


### [173] [Sphere Encoder 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02208)
*Kaiyu Yue,Sean McLeish,Ruchit Rawal,Brian Bartoldson,Menglin Jia,Tom Goldstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sphere Encoder 2，针对原始 Sphere Encoder 生成质量的两大局限（潜在球赤道附近区域训练覆盖不足、像素级重建损失导致图像模糊）进行改进，在保持自编码器速度与简洁性的同时显著提升生成质量，并开源了模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 Sphere Encoder 通过从高维潜在球面解码随机点来生成图像，但其生成质量受两个问题制约：一是随机点相对极点集中在赤道附近，而训练时的旋转始终无法覆盖该区域，形成空白区，限制了单步生成效果；二是以像素级重建损失进行生成训练会促使解码器对多个合理图像取平均，从而产生缺乏高频细节的模糊图像。因此需要一种方法同时解决覆盖不足与模糊问题。

Method: 提出 Sphere Encoder 2，针对上述两个局限进行改进：一方面解决训练旋转未覆盖赤道附近潜在区域导致的覆盖空白问题，另一方面缓解像素级重建损失引起的解码器平均化与模糊问题。整体架构保持自编码器的速度与简洁性。

Result: 相比原始 Sphere Encoder，Sphere Encoder 2 在保持自编码器速度与简洁性的同时，显著提升了图像生成质量。模型已在 GitHub 开源。

Conclusion: Sphere Encoder 2 通过解决潜在空间训练覆盖不足与像素级重建损失导致的模糊问题，有效提升了基于潜在球面解码的自编码器生成质量，并保持了高效简洁的特点。

Abstract: Sphere Encoder is an autoencoder that generates images by decoding random points from a high-dimensional latent sphere. We identify two limitations of the original formulation that reduce its generation quality. First, random points concentrate near the equator relative to the pole on an encoded latent, but the training rotation never reaches this region, leaving a gap that limits one-step generation. Second, training for generation with pixel-wise reconstruction loss encourages the decoder to average over plausible images, producing blurry images that lack high-frequency details. We present Sphere Encoder 2 to address both limitations, substantially improving image generation quality while maintaining the speed and simplicity of a autoencoder. Models are released at \href{https://github.com/kaiyuyue/sphere2}{github.com/kaiyuyue/sphere2}.

</details>


### [174] [Moore, Escher, Penrose: A Conformal Golden Braid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02210)
*Sophia Feldman,Assaf Shocher*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种将生成过程与几何变换"编织"的采样方法，借助冻结的文本到图像扩散模型与非可逆变换的广义逆，在采样过程中协同发展场景与其自指扭曲，生成类似埃舍尔《Print Gallery》的自指构图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 埃舍尔1956年石版画《Print Gallery》呈现自指递归结构，其后有研究用共形幂映射将之与"未扭曲"源图像关联。作者希望用现成扩散模型自动生成此类自指场景，但发现单纯提示无法产生递归；事后对成品图像做变换会使结构连接劣化；在采样中直接施加变换又会被去噪器"修复"掉扭曲或漂离既定几何。因此需要一种能同时生成场景与其几何畸变的方法。

Method: 作者构造了非可逆图像变换T的广义逆T†，并适配其递归约束；理想情形下Penrose恒等式TT†T=T使TT†成为投影到几何可行图像集的幂等算子。由于仅对变换后图像去噪仍属分布外任务，即使加了投影也不理想，故将去噪步骤与T、T†交替"编织"：在源空间步骤发展未扭曲场景，在变换空间步骤精修其外观与最终几何中的连接。基于冻结的文本到图像扩散模型实现。

Result: 方法能够生成类似《Print Gallery》的自指构图，并可推广到其他变换。对比显示，仅靠提示不含递归，事后变换连接差，采样中施加变换会被去噪器修复或漂离几何；而交替编织T与T†的采样方式使场景与其扭曲在生成过程中同步成形，改善了结构性连接。

Conclusion: 与其先完成图像再施加畸变，不如让场景与其畸变在采样过程中共同发展；通过广义逆与去噪步骤的编织，可在冻结扩散模型上实现自指与几何一致的图像生成，并拓展至更多变换类型。

Abstract: I don't think I have ever done anything as peculiar in my life. Among other things, it shows a young man looking with interest at a print on the wall of an exhibition that features himself. How can this be? Perhaps I am not far removed from Einstein's curved universe.'' So wrote M.C. Escher about his 1956 lithograph Print Gallery. Nearly half a century later, a mathematical analysis related its geometry to an untwisted source image through a conformal power map $z \mapsto z^α$, $α\in \mathbb{C}$. Building on this construction, we use a frozen text-to-image diffusion model to generate new self-referential scenes. Prompting alone does not enforce the recursion, while a post-hoc transformation can leave structures poorly connected. Applying the transformation during sampling is also insufficient: the denoiser may "repair" the intended distortion or drift out of the prescribed geometry. We construct a generalized inverse $T^\dagger$ of the non-invertible image transformation $T$, adapted to its recursive constraint. In the idealized formulation, the Penrose identity $TT^\dagger T = T$ makes $TT^\dagger$ an idempotent projection onto geometrically admissible images. Yet denoising only the transformed image remains an out-of-distribution task, even with projection. We therefore braid denoising steps with $T$ and $T^\dagger$: source-space steps develop the untwisted scene, while transformed-space steps refine its appearance and connections in the final geometry. We generate Print Gallery-like compositions and explore further transformations. Rather than distorting a finished image, we let the scene and its distortion develop together.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Supporting Perspective Acquisition and Opinion Formation on Societal Issues Through AI-Generated Japanese Rap Battle Debates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01179)
*Ryota Mibayashi,Toru Urakawa,Dai Takanashi,Tomoya Morohoshi,Kanata Yamagishi,Ryuho Sekikawa,Yasuhiko Nishimura,Yuta Takeuchi,Hideaki Tamori,Takehiro Yamamoto,Hidenari Kiyomitsu,Hiroaki Ohshima*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 研究用GPT-5.4-mini自动生成约1.5分钟的AI说唱对战式辩论（含合成语音与网页界面），通过6名被试、40个社会议题的用户实验，检验其对观点获取与意见形成的教育效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获取多元视角并形成有依据的观点对社会议题的批判性思维与决策至关重要；观察对立观点辩论虽有助于视角获取，但传统辩论耗费大量时间与人力，可及性受限。

Method: 借助GPT-5.4-mini自动生成说唱对战式辩论（押韵诗句承载对立观点，兼具趣味性与论证对话结构），并以合成音频在网页浏览界面呈现；招募6名参与者，观看向日本初高中辩论锦标赛社会议题生成的40段辩论，进行用户研究评估教育效果。

Result: 观看约1.5分钟的说唱对战式辩论后，参与者识别出的观点与讨论要点数量近乎翻倍；在受评估案例中，52.5%的情况下参与者重新审视或改变了自身立场。

Conclusion: 即使是简短的AI生成说唱对战式辩论，也能有效支持社会议题上的视角获取与意见形成。

Abstract: Acquiring diverse perspectives and forming informed opinions on societal issues are essential for critical thinking and informed decision-making. Although observing debates between opposing viewpoints can promote perspective acquisition, conventional debates require substantial time and human resources, limiting their accessibility. This study investigates whether brief AI-generated rap battle-style debates can support perspective acquisition and opinion formation on societal issues. Rap battles are debate-style performances in which opposing viewpoints are expressed through rhymed verses, making them engaging while preserving the structure of argumentative dialogue. To conduct this investigation, we automatically generated rap battle-style debates using GPT-5.4-mini and presented them to participants with synthesized audio through a web-based viewing interface. We evaluated the educational effectiveness of this approach through a user study involving six participants who viewed 40 debates generated from topics on societal issues used in the All Japan Junior and Senior High School Debate Championships. The results showed that watching approximately 1.5 minute rap battle-style debates nearly doubled the number of opinions and discussion points identified by participants. Furthermore, participants reconsidered or changed their stances in 52.5% of the evaluated cases. These findings demonstrate that even brief AI-generated rap battle-style debates can effectively support perspective acquisition and opinion formation on societal issues.

</details>
